## 🧭 Reflow Risk (WCAG 1.4.10)

Page-level indicator: resizes to 320 CSS pixels wide and checks for horizontal overflow. This is a risk indicator, not a conformance verdict — see [Behavioral Accessibility Automation](https://github.com/mgifford/ACCESSIBILITY.md/blob/main/examples/BEHAVIORAL_ACCESSIBILITY_AUTOMATION.md) for what it can and cannot detect.

| Page | Result | Details | Page Title |
|---|---|---|---|
| [View Page](https://www.un.org/ar/) | potential | Page may have horizontal overflow, but test inconclusive | https://www.un.org/ar/ |
| [View Page](https://www.un.org/zh/) | failed | Horizontal overflow detected: 15px of content overflowed the viewport at 320px width | 联合国 \| 健康地球上的和平、尊严与平等 |
| [View Page](https://www.un.org/en/) | failed | Horizontal overflow detected: 100px of content overflowed the viewport at 320px width | https://www.un.org/en/ |

## 🔍 Cross-Page Patterns: Common HTML Issues

These HTML patterns cause the same accessibility errors across multiple pages. **Fix the pattern once in your codebase to fix it everywhere!**

### 🎯 Top Patterns to Fix (Highest Impact)

#### Pattern 1: Affects 7 page(s) - 7 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: ALFA
**Rule**: [SIA-R4: The html element has a non-empty lang attribute](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r4)
**Issue**: The `lang` attribute is either missing, empty, or only whitespace

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<html>...</html>
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
/html
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('/html')`
5. The element will be highlighted

**Affected Pages**:
- https://www.un.org/
- https://www.un.org/en/events/global-assets/bootstrap-3.3.5-dist/css/bootstrap.min.css
- https://www.un.org/en/events/global-assets/bootstrap-3.3.5-dist/css/bootstrap-theme.min.css
- https://www.un.org/en/events/global-assets/css/flying-focus.css
- https://www.un.org/fr/images/splash/animate.css
- *...and 2 more page(s)*

#### Pattern 14: Affects 6 page(s) - 6 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: ALFA
**Rule**: [SIA-R12: Button elements have an accessible name](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r12)
**Issue**: The button does not have an accessible name

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<button role="button" class="owl-dot active">...</button>
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
/button[@class="owl-dot active"]
```

3. Go to Console tab

#### Pattern 15: Affects 6 page(s) - 6 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: ALFA
**Rule**: [SIA-R53: Headings do not skip heading levels in the document outline](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r53)
**Issue**: The heading skips one or more levels

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<h4 class="field-content mosaic">...</h4>
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
/h4[@class="field-content mosaic"]
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('/h4[@class="field-content mosaic"]')`
